# Formula confirm, frozen 7.5dd 5pp · 12 × $26,667 · ls · at and one-knob neighbors

**Run:** 2026-11-02 23:00 UTC+2
**Period:** 2026-01-02 → 2026-10-26 · 297 days (205 trading)
One book of $320,000. The lot count splits that cash. Ranked by **return on avg invested**. 7062 combos. Trusted set is n≥30 (5676 cells).

648 buys × 11 sells. One pass. Each cell is one book size, one loss dropdown, and one profit protect. Stops run first. A full book then sells the biggest loss, or the least profitable winner when that lot is the weaker signal.

## Validation score

A formula is **held** only when January–October has at least 30 signals and 30 symbols, both halves make money, and at least 4 of the 5 disjoint two-month blocks make money. Those blocks are Jan–Feb, Mar–Apr, May–Jun, Jul–Aug, and Sep–Oct. They do not share a month. Rank among held formulas is January–October return on invested. Top 5 share is the best five symbols' profit divided by the full-window stock P&L. The single best cell further down is a different ranking.

Held formulas: **55** of 396.

## Notes

- Anchor sell: 7.5% loss dropdown, 5% of the peak gain, 12 lots of $26,667, ls, strength formula at. Each other sell moves one knob: loss dropdown 5% or 10%, profit protection 2.5%, 7.5%, or 10%, lot count 8, 16, or 32, sell pick dls, or strength formula a.
- Buys are analyst >2, >3, and >4 at projected >0/10/20/30% on yahoo_and_tipranks_1d_recency, yahoo_and_tipranks_2d_recency, and yahoo_and_tipranks_3d_recency.
- Windows are a flat list: the ten months, five disjoint two-month blocks (Jan–Feb, Mar–Apr, May–Jun, Jul–Aug, Sep–Oct), Jan–May, Jun–Oct, and January–October.
- A formula is held when January–October has at least 30 signals and 30 symbols, both halves are profitable, and at least 4 of the 5 disjoint blocks are profitable.
- Each cell keeps signals dated inside that window and prices through the window's last day. A lot still open on that day stays open.
- A ticker that already has an open position is skipped. No SPY and no TQQQ.
- 3 books × 3 analyst cuts × 4 upside cuts × 18 windows × 11 sells = 7,128 cells. Trusted cells have at least 30 signals.
